### Q1. JEE Main 2023 (15 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

Let the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> contain the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn></math> and be parallel to the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math>. Then the distance of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>8</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> measured parallel to the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to _________.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **26**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Let the Plane containing the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn></math> is given by,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Also, this plane is parallel to the line</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Now normal of the plane will be perpendicular to the line, so using perpendicular condition we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mn>4</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>18</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>So, equation of the Plane will be,</p><p><math src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/984044bf-426b-495d-b7e4-ee0523f90945.png" style="width: 350px; height: 243px;" /></p><p>Now to find <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math>,</p><p>Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow><mn>12</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi></math></p><p>So, the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>And <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> lies on plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math>.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mfenced><mrow><mn>12</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>27</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>21</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>56</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>36</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>120</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>190</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>27</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>105</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>210</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Hence, Distance is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>36</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>64</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>576</mn></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>676</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>26</mn></math></p><p>Hence this is the required answer.</p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q2. JEE Main 2023 (15 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let the foot of perpendicular of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> on the plane passing through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>. Then the distance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math> from the origin is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>42</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>38</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>35</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>29</mn></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>42</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given that three points lie on a plane,</p><p> </p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/752b0044-04e1-4dd4-8e2e-a1dbefdf5b88.png" style="width: 270px; height: 242px;" /></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>10</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>10</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>7</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>20</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>30</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>50</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>Equation of plane will be,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi></math></p><p>And the plane passing through, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>15</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math></p><p>Now finding the foot of perpendicular we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mfrac><mn>38</mn><mn>38</mn></mfrac></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Distance from origin <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>25</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn></msqrt></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>42</mn></msqrt></math></p><p>Hence this is the required option.</p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q3. JEE Main 2023 (13 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The plane, passing through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math> and parallel to the line passing through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>)</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></math>also passes through the point

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>B</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and given plane is parallel to line passing through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>So, normal vector to the plane is given by,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" satisfying the points from option in the plane we get, </p><p><math <p>Let the image of the point<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>8</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> in the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>.</mo></math> If the distance of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>,</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>13</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi></math> is equal to<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>_</mi><mi>_</mi><mi>_</mi><mi>_</mi><mi>_</mi><mi>_</mi><mi>_</mi><mo>.</mo></math></p>

**Correct Value:** **15**

xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>8</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math ### Q5. JEE Main 2023 (13 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>Let the equation of plane passing through the line of intersection of the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>11</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo></math> For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8484;</mi><mo>,</mo></math> if the distance of this plane from the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mi>a</mi></msqrt></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mi>c</mi></mfrac></math> is equal to</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Let the equation of plane passing through the intersection of two given planes:</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>This is same as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>21</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>21</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>17</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>17</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow><mi>b</mi></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#8801;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Distance<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mi>a</mi></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math></p><p>Plane<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>:</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>17</mn></math></p><p>The distance of the plane from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>15</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><mi>c</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>17</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>147</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>|</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>|</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8484;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p>Hence this is the required option.</p></blockquote>

### Q6. JEE Main 2023 (12 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

Let the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math> be rotated by an angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> about its line of intersection with the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi></math> is the distance of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math> from the new position of the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>35</mn><mi>&#945;</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>85</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>105</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>126</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>90</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>126</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>We know that if a plane passes through the line of intersection of planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> then the family of planes is given by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math>.</p><p>Equation of plane after rotation :</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Now angle between the new plane and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>90</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></math>.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#183;</mo><msub><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Equation of plane : <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>26</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Distance of plane from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math open="|"><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>40</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>26</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>25</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>100</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>54</mn><mn>15</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>35</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>35</mn><mo>&#183;</mo><mfrac><mn>54</mn><mn>15</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>54</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>126</mn></math></p><p>Hence this is the required option.</p>

### Q7. JEE Main 2023 (11 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> be the plane passing through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>13</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>. For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8469;</mi></math>, if the distance of the points<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>A</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> from the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math> respectively, then the positive value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> be the plane passing through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>13</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p> </p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d2ef36a6-d0d1-4926-acbb-df61af36a374.png" style="width: 250px; height: 196px;" /></p><p>Now, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> ,</p><p>So, normal vector to the plane will be,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>8</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>24</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>Now equation of plane will be: <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>24</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi></math> </p><p>And given plane passes through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>24</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math></p><p>Hence, equation of plane will be,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>24</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p>Now using the formula distance of point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> from the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>, we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>144</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p>Now again using the distance of point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> from the plane formula we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>13</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>39</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>48</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q8. JEE Main 2023 (11 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

If the equation of the plane that contains the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>)</mo></math> and is perpendicular to each of the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>97</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>15</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>18</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>16</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>17</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>15</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given</p><p>The equation of the plane that contains the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and is perpendicular to each of the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>97</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> </p><p>Now satisfying the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> in plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>97</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>97</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mi>i</mi></mfenced></math></p><p>And using perpendicular condition <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>97</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Similarly, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>i</mi><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now solving equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>i</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>i</mi><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>22</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>22</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q9. JEE Main 2023 (11 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>)</mo></math> be the image of point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>)</mo></math> in the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>. Then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>.</p><p>Since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is the image point of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> in the given plane, so</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mn>1</mn></mstyle></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mstyle></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mn>1</mn></mstyle></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mstyle></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mn>1</mn></mstyle></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mstyle></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math></p><p>Hence this is the required option.</p></blockquote>

### Q10. JEE Main 2023 (10 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `3`

Let the foot of perpendicular from the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> on the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>N</mi></math>. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>,</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>&#8484;</mi></math> is a point on plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> such that the area of the triangle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>N</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>&#946;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi></math> is equal to _____________

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **7**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given,</p><p>The foot of perpendicular from the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> on the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>N</mi></math></p><p>So, by foot of perpendicular formula we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>N</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>And <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>&#8484;</mi></math> is a point on plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> such that the area of the triangle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>N</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>,</p><p>So putting the value of point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> in plane we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math></p><p>And given area of triangle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>N</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>, we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>A</mi><mi>N</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#215;</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>N</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>N</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#215;</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>N</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>N</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>N</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>5</mn><msup><mi>&#946;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>18</mn><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mtext>as</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mtext>as&#160;</mtext><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>So,  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>&#946;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math></p></blockquote>

### Q11. JEE Main 2023 (10 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let the image of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></math>in the plane passing through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#947;</mi><mo>)</mo></math> . - **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>70</mn></math>

<blockquote><p>Plane passes through the points</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>We know that the equation of a plane passing through three non-collinear points <math is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" ### Q12. JEE Main 2023 (10 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let two vertices of a triangle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, and its centroid be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>. If the image of the third vertex in the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mi>&#945;</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>70</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>76</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>74</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>72</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>74</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given,</p><p>Two vertices of a triangle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, and its centroid be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now, let the vertex &#39;<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math>&#39; be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and using centroid formula we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p>So, the third vertex will be, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now finding image of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math> in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn></math> we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p>Now find the required answer we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>24</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>74</mn></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q13. JEE Main 2023 (08 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> be the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> be the plane passing through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>, and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>. If the foot of the perpendicular drawn from the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math> on the line of intersection of the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>)</mo></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **11**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> be the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn></math>,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> be the plane passing through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>, and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>,</p><p>Now finding the equation of plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>, we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p>Now direction ratio of line of intersection of planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> is given by,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo></math></p><p>Or direction ratio is given by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>Now finding the point on the line of intersection by taking <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> in both plane,</p><p>We get,  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p>Now solving above two lines we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math> is a point on line of intersection</p><p>Hence, the equation of line will be,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math></p><p>Now any point on the line is given by,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Now finding the distance of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>7</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from the line,
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/1f24eb93-a040-4f5f-b0f4-b9118f3bdc7c-image.png" style="width: 300px; height: 156px;" />
Now from above diagram using perpendicular condition we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>14</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>Hence, the point is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>7</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and comparing with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> we get, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q14. JEE Main 2023 (08 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> be the plane passing through the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>7</mn></mfrac></math> and the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math>. If the image of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math> in the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>)</mo></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>11</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Given,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> be the plane passing through the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mn>7</mn></mfrac></math> and the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math>,</p><p>So, equation of plane is given by,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>7</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>7</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>20</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mn>5</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Now finding the image<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>  of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> with respect to plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math></p></blockquote>

</details>
