accent="true"><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8869;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math we get</p><p><math ### Q67. JEE Main 2021 (18 Mar Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let plane parallel to given plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Distance from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></math></p><p>On comparing with given equation</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>K</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p>

</details>

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>d</mi></math> are integers, then the minimum value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>d</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> is

<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **28**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/358656a3-4fb5-4fe6-a03d-cdf0598cb327-image.png" style="width: 175px; height: 154px;" /></p><p>Mid-point of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> which lies on the plane.</p><p>Direction ratios of normal vector to the plane are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Equation of Plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>d</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi>min</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>28</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q69. JEE Main 2021 (17 Mar Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `3`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> be an arbitrary point having sum of the squares of the distance from the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>l</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> equal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9</mn></math> units. If the locus of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>n</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The distance of a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> from a plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>d</mi></mrow></mfenced><msqrt><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Given the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is an arbitrary point such that the sum of the squares of its distance from the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>l</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is equal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9</mn></math> units.</p><p>Let, the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then we have </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>l</mi><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><msqrt><msup><mi>l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math></p><p>Thus, the locus can be obtained by replacing <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and hence the locus is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>l</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>z</mi><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>ln</mi><mi>x</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><msup><mi>l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><msup><mi>l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>z</mi><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>n</mi><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Since, it is given that the locus is <math ### Q70. JEE Main 2021 (17 Mar Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If the equation of the plane passing through the line of intersection of the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></math> is _________.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Required plane is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>p</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><msub><mi>p</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>;</mo></math></p><p>which is satisfied by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Thus, plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>15</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>47</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>28</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q71. JEE Main 2021 (17 Mar Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The equation of the plane which contains the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi></math>-axis and passes through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/cca95ec3-4c57-4d17-b922-6d35ffd74abe-image.png" style="width: 255px; height: 170px;" /></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mo>(</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q72. JEE Main 2021 (16 Mar Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

<p>If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> be an arbitrary point lying on a plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> which passes through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>42</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>42</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo></math>and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>42</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>14</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math> is<gwmw style="display:none;"></gwmw></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>39</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>45</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Plane passing through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>42</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>42</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>42</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>From ### Q75. JEE Main 2021 (26 Feb Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi></math> be a line obtained from the intersection of two planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>. If point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is the foot of perpendicular from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> on <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>21</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> equals:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>102</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>142</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>68</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>136</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>102</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>On solving, equations <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math>, we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi></math></p><p>Hence, Equation of the line is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> line of intersection of two planes is</p><p><math ### Q76. JEE Main 2021 (26 Feb Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> be a point on the plane which passes through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>. If the plane is perpendicular to the line joining the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>21</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>29</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>23</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mi>&#955;</mi><mn>11</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mn>11</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math> is equal to ________.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **8**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/9f6bd076-797b-4622-86ee-9ee18c1da1e4-4504a8a2-5b97-4497-83b9-ae1a395f0b2d-image.png" style="width: 282px; height: 200px;" /></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">i</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">j</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">k</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">j</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">k</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>22</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mi>&#955;</mi><mn>11</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mn>11</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q77. JEE Main 2021 (26 Feb Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the three planes<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>15</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>21</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo></math> and</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>14</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p><p>Then, which one of the following is true?</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> are parallel.
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> all are parallel.
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> are parallel.
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> are parallel.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> are parallel.**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>7</mn></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> are parallel.</p>

</details>

### Q78. JEE Main 2021 (25 Feb Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

A plane passes through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi>B</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>O</mi></math> is the origin and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the projection of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> on this plane is of length:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>7</mn></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>11</mn></mfrac></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>11</mn></mfrac></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/0bb54168-936e-4a98-872d-2e84f150254f-image.png" style="width: 328px; height: 200px;" /></p><p>Normal to plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>cos</mi><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt><msqrt><mn>11</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>8</mn><msqrt><mn>66</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>66</mn></mfrac></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Projection of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> on plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>|</mo><mover><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>11</mn></mfrac></msqrt></math></p><p>option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>4</mn></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q79. JEE Main 2021 (24 Feb Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The equation of the plane passing through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and perpendicular to the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>11</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>17</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>38</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>11</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>17</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>38</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Normal vector of the plane will be<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>11</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>17</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>So, the direction ratios of normal to the required plane are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>11</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>17</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and plane passes through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>. So, the equation of plane is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>11</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>17</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>11</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>17</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>38</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q80. JEE Main 2020 (06 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The shortest distance between the lines <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>0</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math>and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>We know that shortest distance between two skew lines exists along the line which is perpendicular to both the lines.</p><p>Now finding the equation of a plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mfenced></math>, whose normal is perpendicular to the given first line and the line obtained by planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>.</p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> is:</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>It should be parallel to given line. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Thus, Plane, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>is</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d73de4df-2d6e-43a8-a303-4f455e0dd47b-image.png" style="width: 307px; height: 240px;" /></p><p>So, the shortest distance between the lines will be same as distance between the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> and given line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>0</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mstyle></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mi>z</mi></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mn>1</mn></mstyle></mfrac></math></p>Shortest distance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo></math> Perpendicular distance of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math> from this plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>|</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>|</mo></mrow><msqrt><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q81. JEE Main 2020 (04 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

If the equation of a plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math>, passing through the intersection of the planes, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn></math> for some <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the distance of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Equation of plane of passing through intersection of planes <math      .....<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>7</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>     .......<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>3</mn></mfenced></math></p><p><math ........<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>4</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>On comparing <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>3</mn></mfenced></math> ### Q82. JEE Main 2020 (03 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The plane which bisects the line joining the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> at right angles also passes through the point :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/c9ca4b24-6324-4819-8b23-6cdd72041962-image.png" style="width: 277px; height: 155px;" /></p><p>Mid point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Normal of plane is along the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math>.</p><p>Direction ratio&#39;s of normal is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#62;</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#62;</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#62;</mo></math></p><p>Equation of plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Only option <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>B</mi></mfenced></math> satisfies and is the answer.</p>

</details>

### Q83. JEE Main 2020 (03 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The foot of the perpendicular drawn from the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> to the line joining the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> lies on the plane

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Equation of a line through given points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> will be</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>0</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math>.</p><p>Direction ratio of line is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>.</p><p>A point on this line can be taken as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>.</p><p>Let this point is foot of perpendicular from point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>.</p><p>Direction ratio of line joining these points is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>.</p><p>Both lines are perpendicular to each other.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math>Dot product of directions ratio will be zero.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>18</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>Replace value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#955;</mi></math> for foot of perpendicular.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo></math>Foot of perpendicular is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>.</p><p>This point satisfies the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>.</p>

</details>

### Q84. JEE Main 2020 (02 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

A plane passing through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> contains two lines whose direction ratios are<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></math>and<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn></math> respectively. If, this plane also passes through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> </p><p>Direction ratios of lines are<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>.</p><p>Since both lines lie in to the plane implies dot product of direction ratios of line with direction ratio of normal of plane will be zero.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>a</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>b</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>c</mi><mn>7</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mi>a</mi></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mn>4</mn></mstyle></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mi>b</mi></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mstyle></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mi>c</mi></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mstyle></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Direction ratios of normal of plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Equation of plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> lies on plane</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q85. JEE Main 2020 (02 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The plane passing through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and parallel to the line, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> also passes through the point

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Equation of plane passes through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>It also passes through  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Given line can be written as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>0</mn></mfrac></math> </p><p>Now this line is parallel to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math></p><p>Solve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>a</mi><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>b</mi><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>c</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>a</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>b</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>c</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>b</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>c</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>Plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

### Q86. JEE Main 2020 (08 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The mirror image of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, in a plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math> Which of the following points lies on this plane?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Assume, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math> be the midpoint of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> &amp; <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>So, coordinate of </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mstyle></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mstyle></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mstyle></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>The direction ratio of normal to the plane is </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>10</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>10</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mn>10</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>So, equation of plane is <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>.</p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> lies on this plane.</p>

</details>

### Q87. JEE Main 2020 (07 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `2`

If the foot of the perpendicular drawn from the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> on a line passing through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>7,1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>17</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>, then <math><mi>&#945;</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

Since <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></math>, is perpendicular to <math><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math>, therefore
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/701d37bb-9440-4646-ad5d-48defa15e15e.jpeg" />
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>7</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>17</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>17</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>10</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>98</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>112</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>24</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>.

</details>

### Q88. JEE Main 2020 (07 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Let <math><mi>P</mi></math> be a plane passing through the points <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and R be any point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> .Then the image of <math><mi>R</mi></math> in the plane <math><mi>P</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Solving for Equation of Plane, we get, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p>Now applying relation to find Image of point about a Plane, we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q89. JEE Main 2019 (12 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A plane which bisects the angle between the two given planes <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, passes through the point

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Equation of bisector of angle 
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>±</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>  
<math><mo>(</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>)</mo></math> gives <math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>  
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> gives <math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>  
therefore <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> satisfies the above equation.

</details>

### Q90. JEE Main 2019 (10 Apr Shift 2)

A perpendicular is drawn from a point on the line <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></mfrac></math> to the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> such that the foot of the perpendicular <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math> also lies on the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>. Then the coordinates of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math> are

- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The given line is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo></math>let<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Hence, any point on the line is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Thus, the point <math><mi>P</mi></math> on the line is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>We know that the foot of perpendicular from a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> on the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Thus, the foot of perpendicular <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math> from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> on the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is given by</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Q</mi></math> lies on <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p>On adding the two equations, we get <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Hence, we have <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>So, the coordinate of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math> are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math></p>

</details>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>9</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>15</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>13</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>13</mn></math>**

<p>The distance between the parallel planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>d</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and
