is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><msub><mi>d</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>d</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><msqrt><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Given, the distance between the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>&#955;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> units</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mstyle ### Q92. JEE Main 2019 (10 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

<p>If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math> is the image of the point P in the plane <math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> is the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math>, then the area (in sq. units) of <math><mi>&#916;</mi><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi><mi>R</mi></math> is</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/c56355a2-e405-4f45-b8de-ced06e6308c3-image5.png" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>91</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>91</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>13</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>65</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>91</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, equation of plane is <math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>.
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/c56355a2-e405-4f45-b8de-ced06e6308c3-image5.png" />
Distance, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mn>13</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msqrt></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mi>R</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>R</mi><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi></math> Area of <math><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi><mi>R</mi></math> <math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>P</mi><mi>M</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>R</mi><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mo>&#215;</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>91</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q93. JEE Main 2019 (09 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> be the plane, which contains the line of intersection of the planes, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and it is perpendicular to the <math><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi></math>-plane. Then the distance of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>256</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> is equal to:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>205</mn><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math> units
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>17</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(D)** <math><mn>63</mn><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Using family of planes, equation to plane passing through the line of intersection of the given planes, is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>If a plane is perpendicular to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi></math> plane, then it doesn&#39;t contain the term of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Thus, the plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>The length of perpendicular from a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> to the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>d</mi></mrow></mfenced><msqrt><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mfrac></math></p><p>Hence, required perpendicular distance of point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>256</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> from plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced></math> <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math> units.</p>

</details>

### Q94. JEE Main 2019 (09 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

A plane passing though the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1,</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>0,</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math> and making an angle <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> with the plane <math><mi>y</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> also passes through the point

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1,</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1,</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1,</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The equation of a plane passing through a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> and having direction ratios of the normal to the plane as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>&#62;</mo></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Thus, the equation of the plane passing through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is
<math><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> It passes through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Equation of plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi>i</mi></mfenced></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> It makes angle <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> with plane <math><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>The angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#952;</mi></math> between the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>b</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>c</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>d</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>d</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is defined as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>cos</mi><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>b</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>c</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mrow><msqrt><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mi>b</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mi>c</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></msqrt><mo>&#215;</mo><msqrt><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mrow><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><msqrt><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><mo>&#215;</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><msqrt><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><mo>&#215;</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>Squaring both sides, we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi>b</mi></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Equation of plane is <math><mo>&#177;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mi>&#160;</mi></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Option <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> satisfies the given equation.</p>

</details>

### Q95. JEE Main 2019 (08 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The vector equation of the plane through the line of intersection of the planes <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> which is perpendicular to the plane <math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mo>(</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mo>(</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Planes passing through line of intersection of the planes <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> is
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Since required plane is perpendicular to <math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> hence
<math><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8901;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8901;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8901;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Hence, required plane is <math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> i.e. <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mo>(</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q96. JEE Main 2019 (08 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The equation of a plane containing the line of intersection of the planes <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and passing through the point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi> </mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Equation of plane passing through the intersection of two given Plane <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> It passes through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1,</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Equation of plane <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q97. JEE Main 2019 (12 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Let <math><mi>S</mi></math> be the set of all real values of <math><mi>&#955;</mi></math> such that a plane passing through the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>&#955;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>&#955;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>&#955;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> also passes through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>. Then <math><mi>S</mi></math> is equal to :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>{</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>}</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/1189.png" style="width: 250px; height: 150px;" /></p><p>As we know that if four points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>C</mi></math> are coplanar, the vectors <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover coplanar.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mfenced ### Q98. JEE Main 2019 (11 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

If the point $(2, \alpha, \beta)$ lies on the plane which passes through the points (3,4,2) and (7,0,6) and is perpendicular to the plane $2 x-5 y=15,$ then $2 \alpha-3 \beta$ is equal to :

**Options:**

- **(A)** 12
- **(B)** 7
- **(C)** 5
- **(D)** 17

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) 7**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let the normal to the required plane is $\vec{n}$, then

$\vec{n}=\left|\begin{array}{ccc}\hat{i} & \hat{j} & \hat{k} \\ 4 & -4 & 4 \\ 2 & -5 & 0\end{array}\right|=20 \hat{i}+8 \hat{j}-12$

$\therefore \quad$ Equation of the plane

$(x-3) \times 20+(y-4) \times 8+(z-2) \times(-12)=0$

$5 x-15+2 y-8-3 z+6=0$

$5 x+2 y-3 z-17=0 \quad \ldots(1)$

Since, equation of plane (1) passes through $(2, \alpha, \beta)$, then $10+2 \alpha-3 \beta-17=0 \Rightarrow 2 \alpha-3 \beta=7$

</details>

---

### Q99. JEE Main 2019 (10 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The plane which bisects the line segment joining the points <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>7</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> at right angles, passes through which one of the following points?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The midpoint of line joining the given points is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>  and it lies on the required plane.

Normal to the plane, the line joining the given points, has direction ratio <math><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>10</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mo>&gt;</mo><mo>≡</mo><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>&gt;</mo></math> 

 <math><mo>⇒</mo></math>  equation of plane is  <math><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> 

 <math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn></math> 

Hence plane passes through point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

---

### Q100. JEE Main 2019 (10 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The plane passing through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math> and parallel to the lines <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> also passes through the point

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1,</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> be the normal vector to the plane passing through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math> and parallel to the given lines, then we know that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is perpendicular to both the lines.</p><p>Also, we know that a vector perpendicular to two vectors lie along the cross product of the two vectors.</p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>Thus, the direction ratios of the normal to the plane are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#60;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>&#62;</mo><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>The equation of a plane having direction ratios of the normal to the plane as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>c</mi></math> and passing through a point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>z</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Hence, the equation of the plane having direction ratios the normal to the plane as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#60;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>&#62;</mo></math> and passing through the point<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is </p><p><math><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>From the given options only the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> satisfy the equation of the plane.</p><p>Hence, the plane passes through the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math></p>

</details>

### Q101. JEE Main 2019 (09 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The plane through the intersection of the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and parallel to <math><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo></math> axis also passes through the point

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let required plane is (using concept of family of plane)</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>t</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Again <math><mi>D</mi><mi>R</mi></math> of <math><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo></math> axis is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Since plane is parallel to <math><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo></math> axis</p><p>Hence <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> required plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p>Which satisfy <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q102. JEE Main 2018 (15 Apr Shift 2 Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A plane bisects the line segment joining the points $(1,2,3)$ and $(-3,4,5)$ at right angles. Then this plane also passes through the point.

**Options:**

- **(A)** $(-3,2,1)$
- **(B)** $(3,2,1)$
- **(C)** $(1,2,-3)$
- **(D)** $(-1,2,3)$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $(-3,2,1)$**

**Detailed Solution:**

Since the plane bisects the line joining the points $(1,2,3)$ and $(-3,4,5)$ then the plane passes through the midpoint of the line which is :
$$
\begin{aligned}
\left(\frac{1-3}{2}, \frac{2+4}{2}, \frac{5+3}{2}\right) \equiv\left(\frac{-2}{2},\right.&\left.\frac{6}{2}, \frac{8}{2}\right) \\
& \equiv(-1,3,4) .
\end{aligned}
$$
As plane cuts the line segment at right angle, so the direction cosines of the normal of the plane are $(-3-1,4-2,5-3)=(-4,2,2)$ So the equation of the plane is :
$$
-4 x+2 y+2 z=\lambda
$$
As plane passes through $(-1,3,4)$ so
$$
-4(-1)+2(3)+2(4)=\lambda \Rightarrow \lambda=18
$$
Therefore, equation of plane is :
$$
-4 x+2 y+2 z=18
$$
Now, only $(-3,2,1)$ satisfies the given plane as $-4(-3)+2(2)+2(1)=18$

</details>

---

### Q103. JEE Main 2018 (15 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

An angle between the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and the line of intersection of the planes, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>14</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>17</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>17</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>17</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>17</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>17</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> be the vector along the line of intersection of given planes.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>8</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>24</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>Now, angle between the plane <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and the line of intersection of the planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>14</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is equal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>cos</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn><mi>&#160;</mi></msqrt><mi>&#160;</mi><msqrt><mn>17</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>cos</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><msqrt><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>17</mn></mfrac></msqrt></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>sin</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><msqrt><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>17</mn></mfrac></msqrt></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mo>&#8757;</mo><msup><mi>sin</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>cos</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q104. JEE Main 2018 (15 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

A variable plane passes through a fixed point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and meets <math><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>y</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math>-axes at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>C</mi></math> respectively. A plane is drawn parallel to the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo></math> plane through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math>, a second plane is drawn parallel to the <math><mi>z</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo></math> plane through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> and a third plane is drawn parallel to the <math><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi></math>- plane through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi></math>. Then the locus of the point of intersection of these three planes, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let plane be <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>It passes through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>Now, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Locus of point of intersection of planes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>z</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q105. JEE Main 2018 (15 Apr Shift 1 Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

An angle between the plane, $x+y+z=5$ and the line of intersection of the planes, $3 x+4 y+z-1$ $=0$ and
$5 x+8 y+2 z+14=0$, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** $\cos ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{\sqrt{17}}\right)$
- **(B)** $\cos ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{\frac{3}{17}}\right)$
- **(C)** $\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{\sqrt{17}}\right)$
- **(D)** $\sin ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{\frac{3}{17}}\right)$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $\sin ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{\frac{3}{17}}\right)$**

**Detailed Solution:**

Normal to $3 x+4 y+z=1$ is $3 \hat{i}+4 \hat{j}+k$.
Normal to $5 x+8 y+2 z=-14$ is $5 \hat{i}+8 \hat{j}+2 \hat{k}$
The line of intersection of the planes is perpendicular to both normals, so, direction ratios of the intersection line are directly proportional to the cross product of the normal vectors.
Therefore the direction ratios of the line is $-\hat{j}+4 \hat{k}$
Hence the angle between the plane $x+y+z+5=0$ and the intersection line is $\sin ^{-1}\left(\frac{-1+4}{\sqrt{17} \sqrt{3}}\right)=\sin ^{-1}\left(\sqrt{\frac{3}{17}}\right)$

</details>

---

### Q106. JEE Main 2018 (15 Apr Shift 1 Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

A variable plane passes through a fixed point ( 3 , $2,1)$ and meets $x, y$ and $z$ axes at $A, B$ and $C$ respectively. A plane is drawn parallel to $y z$ - plane through $A$, a second plane is drawn parallel $z x$ plane through $B$ and a third plane is drawn parallel to $x y$ - plane through $C$. Then the locus of the point of intersection of these three planes, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** $(x+y+z=6)$
- **(B)** $\frac{x}{3}+\frac{y}{2}+\frac{z}{1}=1$
- **(C)** $\frac{3}{x}+\frac{2}{y}+\frac{1}{z}=1$
- **(D)** $\frac{1}{x}+\frac{1}{y}+\frac{1}{z}=\frac{11}{6}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) $\frac{3}{x}+\frac{2}{y}+\frac{1}{z}=1$**

**Detailed Solution:**

If $a, b, \mathrm{c}$ are the intercepts of the variable plane on the $x, y, z$ axes respectively, then the equation of the plane is $\frac{x}{a}+\frac{y}{b}+\frac{z}{c}=1$ And the point of intersection of the planes parallel to the $x y, y z$ and $z x$ planes is ( $a, b$, c).
As the point $(3,2,1)$ lies on the variable plane, so $\frac{3}{a}+\frac{2}{b}+\frac{1}{c}=1$
Therefore, the required locus is $\frac{3}{x}+\frac{2}{y}+\frac{1}{z}=1$

</details>

### Q107. JEE Main 2018 (08 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The length of the projection of the line segment joining the points <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math> on the plane, <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/2656562355.jpg" style="width: 300px; height: 150px;" />

DR&#39;s of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><msup><mi>A</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></msup></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>, as this will be normal to the plane.

DR&#39;s of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mrow><msqrt><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><msqrt><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></msqrt><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi></math> also <math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>

Projection <math><msub><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>.</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>.</mo><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mi>&#160;</mi></math>

</details>

### Q108. JEE Main 2018 (08 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

If <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> is the line of intersection of the planes <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is the line of intersection of the planes <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0,</mn></math> then the distance of the origin from the plane, containing the lines <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Direction Ratio&#39;s of the line <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>i</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>j</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>k</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi>i</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>j</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math>

<math><mo>= </mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

At <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mfenced close="]" open="" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math> is point

Equation of <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></math>

Similarly for <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></math>

Point of intersection of <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math>

Direction Ratio&#39;s of normal to plane passing through <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>i</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>j</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>k</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>i</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>j</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>k</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>j</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>7</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

Equation of plane

<math><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi></math> .

<math><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>   (Since plane passes through<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math> )

Equation of plane

<math><mn>7</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

Distance from origin

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mn>3</mn></mfenced><msqrt><msup><mn>7</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mn>8</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>162</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mrow><mn>9</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q109. JEE Main 2017 (09 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

If a variable plane, at a distance of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math> units from the origin, intersects the coordinate axes at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>C</mi></math>, then the locus of the centroid of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let centroid be <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>k</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>l</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>-intercept<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>h</mi><mi>&#160;</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi></math>-intercept<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>k</mi></math></p><p>and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo></math>intercept<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>l</mi></math>

Now, the equation of plane is <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>l</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>.

Distance from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>9</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>9</mn><msup><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>9</mn><msup><mi>l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mi>z</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q110. JEE Main 2017 (08 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The line of intersection of the planes <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></math> is,

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&#215;</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi>&#160;</mi></math>

= <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>-2</mn><mo>i</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>13</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

Now equations of plane are

<math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and

<math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

Let, <math><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>13</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>

<math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and

<math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi>&#8756;</mi></math> Equation of required line is,

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

or

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>13</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q111. JEE Main 2017 (02 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

If the image of the point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> in the plane, <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>22</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> measured parallel to the line, <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi></math> is equal to:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>42</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><msqrt><mn>42</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>42</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Any line parallel to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>y</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>z</mi><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math> and passing through <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math> is</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/644e7b0b-34ee-492d-987f-c5343359b40a-image.png" style="width: 400px; height: 170px;" /></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo></math> (say)</p><p>Any point on above line can be written as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>.</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Coordinates of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>.</mo></math></p><p>Since, point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> lies on the above plane. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>(</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>(</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>22</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>So, point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math>  is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p><p>Now, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mi>R</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>42</mn></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi>P</mi><mi>Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>P</mi><mi>R</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>42</mn></msqrt></math></p><p>Since, point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> lies on the above plane.</p><p>So, point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>)</mo></math></p>

</details>
